- **unversioned-2026-09-27-forseti-the-auto-review-spine**: **Correction (2026-09-27, Forseti's verdict on the spine itself — appended, the original stands).** Two material items, both mended: (1) the anti-double marker (and the review's meta/status/verdict) is keyed to the RESOLVED task state root — never inferred from the existence of `$PROJECT/state`, which seated a second judge under an override (reproduced, then re-run: the marker now holds and the sk ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-09-27-forseti-the-auto-review-spine.md))
- **unversioned-2026-09-27-terminal-act-writes-the-report-shelf**: The 2026-09-27 missed handoff (audited in plan 58, Phase 3): three errands reached `done:` with zero wakes reaching Brokk. Root cause, measured: the spawn brief's status contract (`state/&lt;id&gt;.status`) wakes Brokk only through the live poller, while the handoff failsafe (`bin/agents/eindri-handoff.sh`) sweeps only `$STATE/eindri-reports/` and `$STATE/eindri-questions/` — **never the status shelf**. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-09-27-terminal-act-writes-the-report-shelf.md))
- **unversioned-2026-09-27-the-eindri-push-path-worker-self-wake-and-rearm-catch-up**: The 2026-09-27 missed handoff (audited in plan 58, Phase 3) was only half cured. PR #218 taught the spawn brief's terminal act to write the report shelf the failsafe sweeps, and scheduled the 06:45 sweep. Three decrees of the audit were still open, and the hole was plain: the wake still depended on something else being alive — the poller, the sweep, or the arm. A worker that finished cleanly could ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-09-27-the-eindri-push-path-worker-self-wake-and-rearm-catch-up.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-01-constellation-the-mesh-discovers-peers-and-refuses-to-call-them**: An install of Ymir had no way to know that other installs of Ymir exist. The mesh was a claim, not a surface: `packages/contracts/src/agent-card.ts` defined the card, `packages/a2a/ratatoskr` served one, and nothing on any seat could READ one. So discovery — the cheapest, safest half of a federation — was simply absent. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-01-constellation-the-mesh-discovers-peers-and-refuses-to-call-them.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-04-one-extension-tree-and-a-root-that-walks-up**: The Pi extensions lived in **two** trees, and `bin/seat/valknut-load.sh` copied the second into `&lt;agent-dir&gt;/extensions/`. Pi loads the global and the project directory and does **not** de-duplicate, so the moment the extensions moved into the repo the deployed copy collided with them and **every extension refused to load**: ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-04-one-extension-tree-and-a-root-that-walks-up.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-04-rule-13-and-its-smoke-test**: The layout investigation produced findings but no **law**. Everything it uncovered — the split source, the stale deploy, the two contradictory descriptions, the collision that seats nobody — was a *fact someone had to remember*. A rule makes the memory unnecessary, and a test makes the rule enforceable. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-04-rule-13-and-its-smoke-test.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-04-the-paths-stop-contradicting-themselves**: The Allfather opened with *"why are these extensions in lib?"* — a question the documentation should have answered in one look and could not, because it answered it two ways. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-04-the-paths-stop-contradicting-themselves.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-04-the-tree-mirrors-the-source**: Rule 13 §3 says a multi-file extension is a **directory with an `index.ts`**, and an extension's own internals live inside it. **The tree did that in no places**, and the reason was measurable rather than stylistic: **`lib/` was imported by extensions that had no relationship to the modules.** ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-04-the-tree-mirrors-the-source.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-06-the-errand-door-reported-every-verdict-wrong**: Two failures, both found while filing plan 71, and both of the same species: **a broken thing failed silently, and the silence was shaped exactly like success.** ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-06-the-errand-door-reported-every-verdict-wrong.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-07-the-doctrine-still-described-the-retired-two-home-design**: **Dated:** 2026-10-07 · **Component:** agents (Pi extension surface) · **Branch:** `docs/pi-extensions-one-home` ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-07-the-doctrine-still-described-the-retired-two-home-design.md))

- **unversioned-2026-10-10-private-leaks-and-the-excalidraw-door-moves-to-uncommon-ports**: A batch of small, related corrections carried in the working tree: ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-10-private-leaks-and-the-excalidraw-door-moves-to-uncommon-ports.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-11-four-of-five-copies-of-the-vault-resolver-returned-the-hoard**: **Cites:** `.pi/extensions/ymirhome.ts:8` — the one copy that learned this on 2026-10-08, and wrote the lesson in its own comment. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-11-four-of-five-copies-of-the-vault-resolver-returned-the-hoard.md))

- **unversioned-a-worktree-cannot-corrupt-the-shared-skill-index**: The skill index is **one file in the home** (`$YMIR_HOME/.agents/skills/skills.yaml`), shared by every checkout on the machine. But `skill-index.sh` and `skill-find.sh` resolved the `ships`/`local` shelves from `$ROOT` — whichever checkout was running. Run from a Yggdrasil worktree, that is *the worktree's* shipped skills, so: ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-a-worktree-cannot-corrupt-the-shared-skill-index.md))
- **unversioned-brokk-send-routes-remotely-the-fleet-injection-floor** ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-brokk-send-routes-remotely-the-fleet-injection-floor.md))
- **unversioned-rule-25-gains-the-instance-that-proves-it**: Rule 25 (*agnostic by construction*) was written on 2026-10-18 from that day's wounds. The next day, the rule found its own instance from the other side: the skill index shipped to heimdall with **192 absolute `/home/&lt;seat&gt;/…` paths**, so on the second machine the finder resolved nothing, `--check` read it as incomplete forever, and the home went dirty every session. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-rule-25-gains-the-instance-that-proves-it.md))

- **unversioned-the-skill-index-is-portable-across-machines**: The skill index **did not work on heimdall**. `bin/agents/skill-index.sh` wrote each skill's `path` as an **absolute** path — `192` of them under `/home/&lt;this-seat&gt;/…` — and the index is committed to the **home repo, which syncs to every machine**. On the second machine every one of those paths was a lie: the finder resolved nothing, `--check` read the index as incomplete forever, and the home wen ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-the-skill-index-is-portable-across-machines.md))

For the @lostgradient/* packages the fix is in-repo (this lab lives in the cinder monorepo and consumes them via workspace:*), so there is no dependency-sync leg - but a defect shipping in a published version still requires driving the changesets release through npm publication before the issue closes; only the lab itself is satisfied at merge. ([stevekinney/cinder](https://github.com/stevekinney/cinder/blob/HEAD/labs/chat-room/.claude/agents/upstream-fixer.md) · ★11)
- **upstream-researcher**: Read-only research agent. Given an upstream app/repo, investigates its runtime, build system, native-module hazards, and DB strategy, and returns a concise FreeBSD "port plan". Use in Phase 1 of port-package so research happens off the main context.
